# SpongeBob SquarePants — parent guide (862 episodes)

Source: https://watchwiththekids.com/spongebob.html
Scoring: violence, sex and language each 1–5; overall = the highest of the three.
Buckets: 860 all clear (1–2), 2 gray area (3), 0 hard pass (4–5).

## S2 E29a — & 1 Toilets

  - This article is a transcript of the SpongeBob SquarePants episode "SpongeBob Meets the Strangler" from season 3, which aired on October 11, 2004.
  - SpongeBob: “[standing in front of the punch clock, waiting for it to reach 8:00] Wait for it...”
  - Squidward: “Another day, another migraine. [laughs sheepishly at the audience, then punches in] Mi... [the scene pauses again]”
  - Officer Nancy: “Don't worry, SpongeBob. He won't be able to strangle you.”
  - SpongeBob: “I tattled on him, and now he wants to strangle me with his diabolical hands!”
  - Strangler: “Perfect. That way no one can hear you being strangled-- I mean, uh, uh, protected. [laughs as the lights grow dim] Perfect. [laughs maniacally]”
